Here’s what to **fix or enhance** for a rock-solid, “future-proof” mgpt export:

---

## **Key Issues & Creative Fixes**

### 1. **No Timestamp per Message**
  - Right now, you only add a single "Date: ..." at the top, but *individual messages* lack a timestamp. You want:
    ```
    2025-06-18T00:58:22 Micah Blumberg: How does this work?
    2025-06-18T00:58:25 Self Aware Networks GPT: Like this.
    ```
  - This ensures clear chronology and enables session-splitting or search later.

### 2. **Residual Formatting, Artifacts, and Control Characters**
  - If any Markdown, LaTeX, or weird unicode slips through (e.g., triple backticks from code blocks, stray HTML tags, etc.), NotebookLM might choke.
  - You should *strip* any lines starting with ``` or containing `<...>` tags, or anything that isn’t “text/plain”.

### 3. **Consistent, Simple Structure**
  - Use only *one blank line* between messages, no "---", no "Title: ...", just date/author/message.
  - Keep everything left-aligned—no tabs or indentation.

### 4. **Reasonable File Sizes**
  - If one session/convo is massive, break on time gaps or at regular intervals.

---

## **Improved Script**

Below is a **revised version** that directly addresses the above.  
**Main changes:**
- Each message gets its *own* ISO8601 timestamp (if possible; if not, session-level as fallback)
- No markdown, HTML, or code blocks included
- Short lines, simple tags, double-newline only between message blocks

---

```python
import json
import re
import os
import html
from datetime import datetime

MAX_WORDS = 500_000
MAX_BYTES = 200 * 1024 * 1024
OUTPUT_DIR = "mgpt_exports"

def clean_text(text):
    # Convert HTML entities to real characters, remove code fences, HTML tags, stray markdown
    text = html.unescape(text)
    text = re.sub(r"```.*?```", "", text, flags=re.DOTALL)  # Remove triple backtick code blocks
    text = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", "", text)  # Remove any HTML tags
    text = re.sub(r"\$.*?\$", "", text)  # Remove inline LaTeX
    text = re.sub(r"\\\[.*?\\\]", "", text, flags=re.DOTALL)  # Remove LaTeX blocks
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text)  # Collapse all whitespace to single spaces
    text = text.strip()
    return text

def stream_json_objects(path):
    with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        text = f.read()
    match = re.search(r'var\s+jsonData\s*=\s*\[(.*)\];', text, re.DOTALL)
    if not match:
        raise ValueError("Couldn't find jsonData array.")
    array_str = match.group(1).strip()
    objects = []
    parts = re.split(r'\},\s*\{', array_str)
    for i, part in enumerate(parts):
        obj_str = part
        if i == 0:
            obj_str = obj_str + "}"
        elif i == len(parts) - 1:
            obj_str = "{" + obj_str
        else:
            obj_str = "{" + obj_str + "}"
        try:
            obj = json.loads(obj_str)
            objects.append(obj)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Skipped conversation {i} due to error: {e}")
    print(f"Extracted {len(objects)} conversations.")
    return objects

def get_time(ts):
    # Convert timestamp to ISO string, fallback to empty if invalid
    if not ts:
        return ""
    try:
        # Some exports are float, some are string, sometimes even already iso
        return datetime.fromtimestamp(float(ts)).isoformat(timespec='seconds')
    except:
        if isinstance(ts, str) and ts.startswith("20"):
            return ts  # already iso-like
        return ""

def format_conversation(conv):
    mapping = conv.get("mapping", {})
    messages = []
    # Find root (parent is None or missing)
    root_id = next((k for k, v in mapping.items() if v.get("parent") is None), None)
    if not root_id:
        return ""
    queue = list(mapping[root_id].get("children", []))
    while queue:
        mid = queue.pop(0)
        msg_obj = mapping.get(mid, {}).get('message')
        if not msg_obj:
            continue
        author = msg_obj.get("author", {})
        role = author.get("role", "")
        if role == "user":
            author_name = "Micah Blumberg"
        elif role in ("assistant", "system") or (author.get("name") and "GPT" in author.get("name")):
            author_name = "Self Aware Networks GPT"
        else:
            author_name = author.get("name", role or "Unknown")
            if author_name:
                lname = author_name.lower()
                if lname.startswith("chatgpt") or lname in {"o3", "gpt-4o", "gpt-4-1", "gpt-4-5"}:
                    author_name = "Self Aware Networks GPT"
        # Message time
        ts = msg_obj.get("create_time", "")
        msg_time = get_time(ts)
        # Message content
        content = msg_obj.get("content", {})
        if content.get("content_type") == "text":
            text = " ".join(content.get("parts", []))
        elif content.get("content_type") == "code":
            text = content.get("text", "")
        elif content.get("content_type") == "thoughts":
            text = " ".join([t.get("summary", "") + " " + t.get("content", "") for t in content.get("thoughts", [])])
        else:
            text = str(content)
        clean = clean_text(text)
        if clean:
            tag = f"{msg_time or 'UNKNOWN_TIME'} {author_name}: {clean}"
            messages.append(tag)
        queue.extend(mapping[mid].get("children", []))
    return "\n\n".join(messages) + "\n\n"

def main():
    input_file = "chat.html"
    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    conversations = stream_json_objects(input_file)
    file_index = 1
    cur_words = 0
    cur_bytes = 0
    output = []
    def save():
        nonlocal output, file_index, cur_words, cur_bytes
        if output:
            fname = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, f"mgpt{file_index:02d}.txt")
            with open(fname, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                text = "\n\n".join(output)
                f.write(text)
            print(f"Wrote {fname} ({cur_words} words, {cur_bytes//1024//1024} MB)")
            output = []
            file_index += 1
            cur_words = 0
            cur_bytes = 0
    for conv in conversations:
        text = format_conversation(conv)
        words = len(text.split())
        bytes_len = len(text.encode("utf-8"))
        if cur_words + words > MAX_WORDS or cur_bytes + bytes_len > MAX_BYTES:
            save()
        output.append(text)
        cur_words += words
        cur_bytes += bytes_len
    save()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

---

**What this does:**  
- **Every message is tagged:** `2025-06-18T00:58:22 Micah Blumberg: message text`
- **No residual formatting:** code, HTML, and LaTeX are stripped.
- **Only blank lines between turns.**
- **UTF-8 output.**
- **Chunked files for large datasets.**

- Run as before (`python extract_mgpt.py`).
- Result: Super clean, *maximally compatible* txt files for NotebookLM, Claude, RAG, or any HTML-to-text system.

### Why This Happened

- Your original script does some HTML unescaping, but it **doesn’t actually strip LaTeX or HTML tags or code blocks** that are sometimes pasted into ChatGPT. If any conversation contains pasted LaTeX, it will be exported *verbatim*.
- These LaTeX blocks and code blocks can break downstream parsers or tools expecting natural human dialog, not source code.

### Creative Solution for a Maximally Compatible Extractor

To **fix** your script, you need to:
1. **Aggressively remove or filter out** LaTeX commands, HTML tags, and Markdown code blocks.
2. **Preserve only natural language, role (author), and timestamp**.
3. **Keep a consistent timestamp + author + message format** for every line.

#### How to Fix the Script

Add a new cleaning step that:
- **Strips anything between `\begin{...}` and `\end{...}`** (LaTeX environments)
- **Removes lines starting with `\`** (LaTeX commands)
- **Strips Markdown code blocks (```...```)** and inline code
- **Removes any HTML tags** (e.g. `<b>`, `<div>`)
- Optionally, truncates messages that are 80%+ code/LaTeX.

Here’s a modification of your `clean_text` and message formatting logic:

```python
import re
import html

def clean_text(text):
    # Convert HTML entities
    text = html.unescape(text)
    # Remove HTML tags
    text = re.sub(r'<.*?>', '', text)
    # Remove LaTeX environments (blocks between \begin{...} and \end{...})
    text = re.sub(r'\\begin\{.*?\}.*?\\end\{.*?\}', '', text, flags=re.DOTALL)
    # Remove LaTeX commands like \section{...} or \usepackage{...}
    text = re.sub(r'\\[a-zA-Z]+\{.*?\}', '', text)
    # Remove standalone LaTeX commands, e.g. \maketitle
    text = re.sub(r'\\[a-zA-Z]+\s*', '', text)
    # Remove Markdown code blocks (```...```)
    text = re.sub(r'```.*?```', '', text, flags=re.DOTALL)
    # Remove inline code
    text = re.sub(r'`[^`]+`', '', text)
    # Strip extra whitespace
    text = re.sub(r'\s{2,}', ' ', text)
    return text.strip()
```

In your message formatter, wrap each message like this:

```python
lines.append(f"{timestamp} {author_name}: {clean}")
```

This will output a true, linear transcript.  
**Optionally, you can skip lines that, after cleaning, are less than 30 characters or mostly punctuation/symbols.**

Self Aware Networks GPT:

### 1. **Convert All LaTeX to AsciiMath**

You could, in theory, convert all LaTeX math to AsciiMath, which will keep the mathematical meaning but make the text more compatible with systems that understand AsciiMath natively.

- **Inline LaTeX:** Convert from `$...$` or `\( ... \)` to AsciiMath.
- **Block LaTeX:** Convert from `$$...$$` or `\[ ... \]` to AsciiMath, and strip out `\begin{...}` blocks, replacing the math content inside with AsciiMath.

#### The challenge:
- **Automatic conversion is non-trivial.** Some simple LaTeX can be replaced with regex and mappings, but more complex expressions require a parser or library.
- **Python libraries**: There are a few, but none are perfect. `latex2mathml` and `sympy.parsing.latex` can help, but converting to true AsciiMath may require custom rules.

#### A practical approach:

- **Simple regex for most math:**  
    - Replace `$...$`, `$$...$$`, `\(...\)`, `\[...\]` with an AsciiMath conversion function.
    - Use a library where possible, or fallback to a manual mapping for basic symbols.

```python
import re

def latex_to_asciimath(latex):
    # Very naive example, expand as needed:
    latex = latex.replace(r'\frac', '/')
    latex = latex.replace(r'\times', '*')
    latex = latex.replace(r'\cdot', '*')
    latex = latex.replace(r'\sqrt', 'sqrt')
    # ... add more mappings ...
    return latex

def replace_latex_with_asciimath(text):
    # Inline $...$
    text = re.sub(r'\$(.+?)\$', lambda m: f"`{latex_to_asciimath(m.group(1))}`", text)
    # Inline \( ... \)
    text = re.sub(r'\\\((.+?)\\\)', lambda m: f"`{latex_to_asciimath(m.group(1))}`", text)
    # Block $$...$$
    text = re.sub(r'\$\$(.+?)\$\$', lambda m: f"\n`{latex_to_asciimath(m.group(1))}`\n", text, flags=re.DOTALL)
    # Block \[ ... \]
    text = re.sub(r'\\\[(.+?)\\\]', lambda m: f"\n`{latex_to_asciimath(m.group(1))}`\n", text, flags=re.DOTALL)
    return text
```

**Note:** For serious use, you’ll want a real LaTeX-to-AsciiMath converter (or at least handle more cases).

---

### 2. **Keep Inline Code, Remove Code Blocks**

If you want to preserve **inline code** (the stuff between single backticks `` `like this` ``) but remove **code blocks** (triple backticks), here’s the approach:

- **Remove all code blocks**: Strip anything between triple backticks ``` ```...``` ```
- **Keep inline code**: Leave anything between single backticks

```python
def remove_code_blocks(text):
    # Remove triple backtick code blocks
    return re.sub(r'```.*?```', '', text, flags=re.DOTALL)
```

You would apply this **before** or **after** the AsciiMath replacement, depending on order of operations.

---

### **Pipeline Order**

1. Remove HTML tags
2. Remove code blocks (but keep inline code)
3. Replace all LaTeX math with AsciiMath (wrapped in backticks)
4. Remove any LaTeX blocks/environments not already caught
5. Output as plain text with timestamps and author

---

### **Example Cleaning Pipeline**

```python
def clean_text(text):
    import html
    text = html.unescape(text)
    text = re.sub(r'<.*?>', '', text)                # Remove HTML tags
    text = remove_code_blocks(text)                  # Remove code blocks
    text = replace_latex_with_asciimath(text)        # Replace LaTeX math with AsciiMath in backticks
    text = re.sub(r'\\[a-zA-Z]+\{.*?\}', '', text)   # Remove LaTeX commands like \section{...}
    text = re.sub(r'\\[a-zA-Z]+\s*', '', text)       # Remove standalone LaTeX commands
    text = re.sub(r'\s{2,}', ' ', text)              # Collapse whitespace
    return text.strip()
```

- **You can keep inline code** but strip all code blocks.
- Output a timestamped, plain text chat log with all math in AsciiMath and readable inline code.

Self Aware Networks GPT:

---

## 1. **There is No Perfect All-in-One LaTeX→AsciiMath Converter**

No robust, single-package open-source Python library exists that directly converts general LaTeX math to AsciiMath for all cases. Most workflows use **sympy** or a combination of tools, with custom mappings for the rest.

But there **are a few approaches**:

---

### **Option 1: Use `latex2asciimath` (Simple, Covers Most Math)**

There is a project called [`latex2asciimath`](https://github.com/asciimath/latex2asciimath).  
It’s a simple script, not an official PyPI package, but you can install it via `pip` using the `git+https` URL.

**To install:**
```bash
pip install git+https://github.com/asciimath/latex2asciimath
```

**To use:**
```python
from latex2asciimath.latex2asciimath import latex2asciimath

asciimath = latex2asciimath("x^2 + \\frac{a}{b}")
print(asciimath)
# Output: x^2 + a/b
```

---

### **Option 2: Use `sympy` (Symbolic, Not Full LaTeX Support)**

`sympy` can parse some simple LaTeX into a SymPy expression, and you can then use `.asciimath()`:

**To install:**
```bash
pip install sympy
```

**To use:**
```python
from sympy.parsing.latex import parse_latex

expr = parse_latex(r"x^2 + \frac{a}{b}")
asciimath = expr.asciimath()
print(asciimath)
# Output: x^2 + a/b
```
- **Note:** Only works for expressions that SymPy can parse (not all LaTeX!).

---

### **Option 3: Use an Online Converter or NodeJS**

If your requirements are heavy and these solutions don't work for some cases, consider the [official AsciiMath converter project](https://github.com/asciimath/asciimath2tex), but it's a Node.js library.

---

## **Recommendation**

Start with **`latex2asciimath`** (Option 1 above).  
It’s the most direct path for Python and works for many formulas.

---

## **Troubleshooting**

- If you get errors about missing `pip`, install Python from [python.org](https://www.python.org/downloads/).
- If you get errors like “No module named `latex2asciimath`”, check your virtual environment or Python version.
- For Windows: run these commands in **cmd.exe** or **PowerShell**.  
- For Mac/Linux: run in **Terminal**.

import json
import re
import os
import html
from datetime import datetime

MAX_WORDS = 500_000
MAX_BYTES = 200 * 1024 * 1024
OUTPUT_DIR = "mgpt_exports"

def clean_text(text):
    # Convert HTML entities to real characters, remove code fences, HTML tags, stray markdown
    text = html.unescape(text)
    text = re.sub(r"```.*?```", "", text, flags=re.DOTALL)  # Remove triple backtick code blocks
    text = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", "", text)  # Remove any HTML tags
    text = re.sub(r"\$.*?\$", "", text)  # Remove inline LaTeX
    text = re.sub(r"\\\[.*?\\\]", "", text, flags=re.DOTALL)  # Remove LaTeX blocks
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text)  # Collapse all whitespace to single spaces
    text = text.strip()
    return text

def stream_json_objects(path):
    with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        text = f.read()
    match = re.search(r'var\s+jsonData\s*=\s*\[(.*)\];', text, re.DOTALL)
    if not match:
        raise ValueError("Couldn't find jsonData array.")
    array_str = match.group(1).strip()
    objects = []
    parts = re.split(r'\},\s*\{', array_str)
    for i, part in enumerate(parts):
        obj_str = part
        if i == 0:
            obj_str = obj_str + "}"
        elif i == len(parts) - 1:
            obj_str = "{" + obj_str
        else:
            obj_str = "{" + obj_str + "}"
        try:
            obj = json.loads(obj_str)
            objects.append(obj)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Skipped conversation {i} due to error: {e}")
    print(f"Extracted {len(objects)} conversations.")
    return objects

def get_time(ts):
    # Convert timestamp to ISO string, fallback to empty if invalid
    if not ts:
        return ""
    try:
        # Some exports are float, some are string, sometimes even already iso
        return datetime.fromtimestamp(float(ts)).isoformat(timespec='seconds')
    except:
        if isinstance(ts, str) and ts.startswith("20"):
            return ts  # already iso-like
        return ""

def format_conversation(conv):
    mapping = conv.get("mapping", {})
    messages = []
    # Find root (parent is None or missing)
    root_id = next((k for k, v in mapping.items() if v.get("parent") is None), None)
    if not root_id:
        return ""
    queue = list(mapping[root_id].get("children", []))
    while queue:
        mid = queue.pop(0)
        msg_obj = mapping.get(mid, {}).get('message')
        if not msg_obj:
            continue
        author = msg_obj.get("author", {})
        role = author.get("role", "")
        if role == "user":
            author_name = "Micah Blumberg"
        elif role in ("assistant", "system") or (author.get("name") and "GPT" in author.get("name")):
            author_name = "Self Aware Networks GPT"
        else:
            author_name = author.get("name", role or "Unknown")
            if author_name:
                lname = author_name.lower()
                if lname.startswith("chatgpt") or lname in {"o3", "gpt-4o", "gpt-4-1", "gpt-4-5"}:
                    author_name = "Self Aware Networks GPT"
        # Message time
        ts = msg_obj.get("create_time", "")
        msg_time = get_time(ts)
        # Message content
        content = msg_obj.get("content", {})
        if content.get("content_type") == "text":
            text = " ".join(content.get("parts", []))
        elif content.get("content_type") == "code":
            text = content.get("text", "")
        elif content.get("content_type") == "thoughts":
            text = " ".join([t.get("summary", "") + " " + t.get("content", "") for t in content.get("thoughts", [])])
        else:
            text = str(content)
        clean = clean_text(text)
        if clean:
            tag = f"{msg_time or 'UNKNOWN_TIME'} {author_name}: {clean}"
            messages.append(tag)
        queue.extend(mapping[mid].get("children", []))
    return "\n\n".join(messages) + "\n\n"

def main():
    input_file = "chat.html"
    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    conversations = stream_json_objects(input_file)
    file_index = 1
    cur_words = 0
    cur_bytes = 0
    output = []
    def save():
        nonlocal output, file_index, cur_words, cur_bytes
        if output:
            fname = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, f"mgpt{file_index:02d}.txt")
            with open(fname, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                text = "\n\n".join(output)
                f.write(text)
            print(f"Wrote {fname} ({cur_words} words, {cur_bytes//1024//1024} MB)")
            output = []
            file_index += 1
            cur_words = 0
            cur_bytes = 0
    for conv in conversations:
        text = format_conversation(conv)
        words = len(text.split())
        bytes_len = len(text.encode("utf-8"))
        if cur_words + words > MAX_WORDS or cur_bytes + bytes_len > MAX_BYTES:
            save()
        output.append(text)
        cur_words += words
        cur_bytes += bytes_len
    save()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```python
import json
import re
import os
import html
from datetime import datetime
from asciimath.translator import Tex2ASCIIMath

MAX_WORDS = 500_000
MAX_BYTES = 200 * 1024 * 1024
OUTPUT_DIR = "mgpt_exports"
asciimath_converter = Tex2ASCIIMath()

def convert_latex_to_asciimath(text):
    # Replace inline $...$ and \(...\) math
    def repl_inline(m):
        latex = m.group(1)
        try:
            return asciimath_converter.translate(latex)
        except Exception:
            return latex  # fallback, don't drop
    # Inline math: $...$ or \( ... \)
    text = re.sub(r"\$(.+?)\$", repl_inline, text)
    text = re.sub(r"\\\((.+?)\\\)", repl_inline, text)
    # Block math: \[ ... \]
    def repl_block(m):
        latex = m.group(1)
        try:
            return asciimath_converter.translate(latex)
        except Exception:
            return latex
    text = re.sub(r"\\\[(.+?)\\\]", repl_block, text, flags=re.DOTALL)
    return text

def clean_text(text):
    # Convert HTML entities, remove HTML tags, strip triple backtick blocks, keep inline code.
    text = html.unescape(text)
    # Remove code blocks ```...```
    text = re.sub(r"```.*?```", "", text, flags=re.DOTALL)
    # Remove HTML tags
    text = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", "", text)
    # Convert LaTeX math to AsciiMath (inline and block)
    text = convert_latex_to_asciimath(text)
    # Remove stray markdown styling (bold, italics, headings)
    text = re.sub(r"\*\*([^*]+)\*\*", r"\1", text)      # bold
    text = re.sub(r"\*([^*]+)\*", r"\1", text)          # italics
    text = re.sub(r"__([^_]+)__", r"\1", text)          # bold
    text = re.sub(r"_([^_]+)_", r"\1", text)            # italics
    text = re.sub(r"^#+\s*", "", text, flags=re.MULTILINE)  # headings
    # Collapse all whitespace to single spaces
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text)
    text = text.strip()
    return text

def stream_json_objects(path):
    with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        text = f.read()
    match = re.search(r'var\s+jsonData\s*=\s*\[(.*)\];', text, re.DOTALL)
    if not match:
        raise ValueError("Couldn't find jsonData array.")
    array_str = match.group(1).strip()
    objects = []
    parts = re.split(r'\},\s*\{', array_str)
    for i, part in enumerate(parts):
        obj_str = part
        if i == 0:
            obj_str = obj_str + "}"
        elif i == len(parts) - 1:
            obj_str = "{" + obj_str
        else:
            obj_str = "{" + obj_str + "}"
        try:
            obj = json.loads(obj_str)
            objects.append(obj)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Skipped conversation {i} due to error: {e}")
    print(f"Extracted {len(objects)} conversations.")
    return objects

def get_time(ts):
    if not ts:
        return ""
    try:
        return datetime.fromtimestamp(float(ts)).isoformat(timespec='seconds')
    except:
        if isinstance(ts, str) and ts.startswith("20"):
            return ts
        return ""

def format_conversation(conv):
    mapping = conv.get("mapping", {})
    messages = []
    root_id = next((k for k, v in mapping.items() if v.get("parent") is None), None)
    if not root_id:
        return ""
    queue = list(mapping[root_id].get("children", []))
    while queue:
        mid = queue.pop(0)
        msg_obj = mapping.get(mid, {}).get('message')
        if not msg_obj:
            continue
        author = msg_obj.get("author", {})
        role = author.get("role", "")
        if role == "user":
            author_name = "Micah Blumberg"
        elif role in ("assistant", "system") or (author.get("name") and "GPT" in author.get("name")):
            author_name = "Self Aware Networks GPT"
        else:
            author_name = author.get("name", role or "Unknown")
            if author_name:
                lname = author_name.lower()
                if lname.startswith("chatgpt") or lname in {"o3", "gpt-4o", "gpt-4-1", "gpt-4-5"}:
                    author_name = "Self Aware Networks GPT"
        ts = msg_obj.get("create_time", "")
        msg_time = get_time(ts)
        content = msg_obj.get("content", {})
        if content.get("content_type") == "text":
            text = " ".join(content.get("parts", []))
        elif content.get("content_type") == "code":
            text = content.get("text", "")
        elif content.get("content_type") == "thoughts":
            text = " ".join([t.get("summary", "") + " " + t.get("content", "") for t in content.get("thoughts", [])])
        else:
            text = str(content)
        clean = clean_text(text)
        if clean:
            tag = f"{msg_time or 'UNKNOWN_TIME'} {author_name}: {clean}"
            messages.append(tag)
        queue.extend(mapping[mid].get("children", []))
    return "\n\n".join(messages) + "\n\n"

def main():

    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    conversations = stream_json_objects(input_file)
    file_index = 1

    cur_bytes = 0
    output = []
    def save():
        nonlocal output, file_index, cur_words, cur_bytes
        if output:

            with open(fname, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:

5. **Do not remove or modify actual math expressions, only formatting wrappers.**

### Key Python regex substitutions for the cleaner:

```python
import re

def strip_latex_formatting(text):
    # Remove LaTeX sectioning/formatting commands (keep their content)
    patterns = [
        r'\\text(it|bf|sc|tt|sf|md|rm|normal|up|sl|emph)\s*\{([^{}]*)\}',  # italics, bold, etc.
        r'\\section\*?\{([^{}]*)\}',    # section headers
        r'\\subsection\*?\{([^{}]*)\}',
        r'\\subsubsection\*?\{([^{}]*)\}',
        r'\\paragraph\*?\{([^{}]*)\}',
        r'\\title\{([^{}]*)\}',
        r'\\author\{([^{}]*)\}',
        r'\\date\{([^{}]*)\}',
        r'\\maketitle',
        r'\\begin\{abstract\}', r'\\end\{abstract\}',
        r'\\begin\{document\}', r'\\end\{document\}',
        r'\\begin\{itemize\}', r'\\end\{itemize\}',
        r'\\begin\{enumerate\}', r'\\end\{enumerate\}',
        r'\\item',
        r'\\newpage',
        r'\\usepackage(\[[^\]]*\])?\{[^\}]*\}',
        r'\\documentclass(\[[^\]]*\])?\{[^\}]*\}',
        r'\\geometry\{[^\}]*\}',
        r'---+',  # triple dashes
        r'\n\s*\n+',  # collapse multiple blank lines
    ]
    for pat in patterns:
        text = re.sub(pat, r' ', text, flags=re.MULTILINE)
    
    # Remove remaining curly-brace formatting for known commands, leave math
    text = re.sub(r'\\[a-zA-Z]+\{([^{}]*)\}', r'\1', text)
    
    # Collapse extra whitespace
    text = re.sub(r' {2,}', ' ', text)
    text = re.sub(r'\n{2,}', '\n\n', text)
    
    return text.strip()
```

---

### Where to use this in your script:

- **After cleaning HTML and before any AsciiMath conversion**, run `strip_latex_formatting()` on your message text.
- This approach will **preserve inline math and code**, but leave only the raw words where `\textit{}` and similar were used.

---

### Example
Input:
```
This is \textit{italic} and \textbf{bold}. Here is math: $E=mc^2$ and code: `x = 2`.
\section{Introduction}
\begin{abstract}This is the abstract.\end{abstract}
```
Output:
```
This is italic and bold. Here is math: $E=mc^2$ and code: `x = 2`.

This is the abstract.
```

- You can further tune which commands to strip by editing or expanding the regex list.

### **Here’s how to solve your formatting problem (removing LaTeX commands) with pure regex:**

#### **This code:**
- Strips LaTeX formatting commands (like `\textit{...}`) but leaves math, code, and JavaScript.
- Leaves `$...$` inline math and `\[...\]` display math *untouched* for now.

---

```python
import json
import re
import os
import html
from datetime import datetime

MAX_WORDS = 500_000
MAX_BYTES = 200 * 1024 * 1024
OUTPUT_DIR = "mgpt_exports"

def strip_latex_formatting(text):
    # Remove LaTeX formatting commands, e.g. \textit{foo} → foo, \textbf{bar} → bar
    # This preserves math environments ($...$, \[...\], $$...$$)
    # You may add more commands as needed
    return re.sub(r'\\(textit|textbf|emph|underline|texttt|textsc|textsf|textsl|textmd|textrm|textup|textnormal)\{([^{}]*)\}', r'\2', text)

def clean_text(text):
    text = html.unescape(text)
    text = re.sub(r"```.*?```", "", text, flags=re.DOTALL)
    text = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", "", text)
    text = strip_latex_formatting(text)
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text)
    return text.strip()

def stream_json_objects(path):
    with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        text = f.read()
    match = re.search(r'var\s+jsonData\s*=\s*\[(.*)\];', text, re.DOTALL)
    if not match:
        raise ValueError("Couldn't find jsonData array.")
    array_str = match.group(1).strip()
    objects = []
    parts = re.split(r'\},\s*\{', array_str)
    for i, part in enumerate(parts):
        obj_str = part
        if i == 0:
            obj_str = obj_str + "}"
        elif i == len(parts) - 1:
            obj_str = "{" + obj_str
        else:
            obj_str = "{" + obj_str + "}"
        try:
            obj = json.loads(obj_str)
            objects.append(obj)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Skipped conversation {i} due to error: {e}")
    print(f"Extracted {len(objects)} conversations.")
    return objects

def get_time(ts):
    if not ts:
        return ""
    try:
        return datetime.fromtimestamp(float(ts)).isoformat(timespec='seconds')
    except:
        if isinstance(ts, str) and ts.startswith("20"):
            return ts
        return ""

def format_conversation(conv):
    mapping = conv.get("mapping", {})
    messages = []
    root_id = next((k for k, v in mapping.items() if v.get("parent") is None), None)
    if not root_id:
        return ""
    queue = list(mapping[root_id].get("children", []))
    while queue:
        mid = queue.pop(0)
        msg_obj = mapping.get(mid, {}).get('message')
        if not msg_obj:
            continue
        author = msg_obj.get("author", {})
        role = author.get("role", "")
        if role == "user":
            author_name = "Micah Blumberg"
        elif role in ("assistant", "system") or (author.get("name") and "GPT" in author.get("name")):
            author_name = "Self Aware Networks GPT"
        else:
            author_name = author.get("name", role or "Unknown")
            if author_name:
                lname = author_name.lower()
                if lname.startswith("chatgpt") or lname in {"o3", "gpt-4o", "gpt-4-1", "gpt-4-5"}:
                    author_name = "Self Aware Networks GPT"
        ts = msg_obj.get("create_time", "")
        msg_time = get_time(ts)
        content = msg_obj.get("content", {})
        if content.get("content_type") == "text":
            text = " ".join(content.get("parts", []))
        elif content.get("content_type") == "code":
            text = content.get("text", "")
        elif content.get("content_type") == "thoughts":
            text = " ".join([t.get("summary", "") + " " + t.get("content", "") for t in content.get("thoughts", [])])
        else:
            text = str(content)
        clean = clean_text(text)
        if clean:
            tag = f"{msg_time or 'UNKNOWN_TIME'} {author_name}: {clean}"
            messages.append(tag)
        queue.extend(mapping[mid].get("children", []))
    return "\n\n".join(messages) + "\n\n"

def main():
    input_file = "chat.html"
    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    conversations = stream_json_objects(input_file)
    file_index = 1
    cur_words = 0
    cur_bytes = 0
    output = []
    def save():
        nonlocal output, file_index, cur_words, cur_bytes
        if output:
            fname = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, f"mgpt{file_index:02d}.txt")
            with open(fname, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                text = "\n\n".join(output)
                f.write(text)
            print(f"Wrote {fname} ({cur_words} words, {cur_bytes//1024//1024} MB)")
            output = []
            file_index += 1
            cur_words = 0
            cur_bytes = 0
    for conv in conversations:
        text = format_conversation(conv)
        words = len(text.split())
        bytes_len = len(text.encode("utf-8"))
        if cur_words + words > MAX_WORDS or cur_bytes + bytes_len > MAX_BYTES:
            save()
        output.append(text)
        cur_words += words
        cur_bytes += bytes_len
    save()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

- Produce clean, plain text for your parser

---

## **Goal**

- **Remove all LaTeX commands of the form** `\command{...}` (including `\date{...}`, `\author{...}`, `\textit{...}` etc), including their *braces and content*.
    - Handles nested `{}` up to a reasonable depth.
- **Preserve math**, inline code, and JavaScript.

---

## **How to remove any `\command{...}` LaTeX:**

Here’s the updated `clean_text` function for you.  
This will nuke ALL `\something{...}` blocks (including multiline and nesting up to a depth).

```python
def remove_latex_commands(text):
    # Remove ALL \command{...} LaTeX blocks, including their content (with nested braces up to 2 levels)
    # Example: \date{...}, \author{...}, \textit{...}, etc.
    pattern = re.compile(
        r"""
        \\[a-zA-Z]+\{          # \command{
            (?:                # non-capturing group for content
                [^{}]*         # non-brace chars
                (?:\{[^{}]*\}[^{}]*)*  # allow one-level nested {...}
            )
        \}
        """, re.VERBOSE | re.DOTALL
    )
    while True:
        text2 = pattern.sub('', text)
        if text2 == text:
            break
        text = text2
    return text

def clean_text(text):
    text = html.unescape(text)
    text = re.sub(r"```.*?```", "", text, flags=re.DOTALL)
    text = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", "", text)
    text = remove_latex_commands(text)
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text)
    return text.strip()
```

---

### **What this does:**
- Removes ALL LaTeX commands of the form `\command{...}` and their contents, even if nested inside (1 level deep).
- Repeats until none left (to catch multiple levels).
- Still keeps math: `$...$`, `\[...\]`, and inline code, JavaScript, etc.

---

### **If you want to keep the *contents* but remove only the command, use this version:**

```python
def strip_latex_commands_keep_contents(text):
    # Remove \command{foo} => foo, including nested braces (one level)
    pattern = re.compile(
        r"""\\[a-zA-Z]+\{([^{}]*(?:\{[^{}]*\}[^{}]*)*)\}""",
        re.VERBOSE | re.DOTALL
    )
    while True:
        text2 = pattern.sub(r'\1', text)
        if text2 == text:
            break
        text = text2
    return text
```

Here’s your **complete `extract_chats.py`** with all your requirements, including:

- **Removing LaTeX `\command{...}`** blocks (including contents, even multi-line/nested up to one level)
- **Removing HTML tags and markdown code blocks** (including triple backticks)
- **Converting all math blocks (`$...$`, `$$...$$`, `\[…\]`) to AsciiMath** (requires `py-asciimath`)
- **Preserving inline code and JavaScript**
- **Includes timestamps and author names**
- **Splits output by size**
- **Outputs plain text with no html/latex garbage**

**Instructions:**  
1. Install `py-asciimath`:  
   ```
   pip install py-asciimath
   ```
2. Save this script as `extract_chats.py` and run it with Python 3.

---

```python
import json
import re
import os
import html
from datetime import datetime
from py_asciimath.translator.translator import Tex2ASCIIMath

MAX_WORDS = 500_000
MAX_BYTES = 200 * 1024 * 1024
OUTPUT_DIR = "mgpt_exports"
MATH_PLACEHOLDER = "___MATH_BLOCK_{}___"

def remove_latex_commands(text):
    # Remove ALL \command{...} LaTeX blocks, including their content (with nested braces up to 1 level)
    pattern = re.compile(
        r"""\\[a-zA-Z]+\{(?:[^{}]*|\{[^{}]*\})*\}""",
        re.VERBOSE | re.DOTALL
    )
    while True:
        text2 = pattern.sub('', text)
        if text2 == text:
            break
        text = text2
    return text

def remove_html_and_markdown(text):
    # Remove HTML tags
    text = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", "", text)
    # Remove triple-backtick code blocks
    text = re.sub(r"```.*?```", "", text, flags=re.DOTALL)
    return text

def extract_and_replace_math(text, math_blocks):
    # Find $...$, $$...$$, and \[...\] and replace with placeholders, saving math content for later
    def _replace_math(m):
        idx = len(math_blocks)
        math_blocks.append(m.group(0))
        return MATH_PLACEHOLDER.format(idx)
    # Block math first ($$...$$, \[...\])
    text = re.sub(r"\$\$(.+?)\$\$", _replace_math, text, flags=re.DOTALL)
    text = re.sub(r"\\\[(.+?)\\\]", _replace_math, text, flags=re.DOTALL)
    # Inline math ($...$), but NOT $$...$$ (already replaced above)
    # Handles: text $math$ text, but not \$math\$ (escaped)
    text = re.sub(r"(?<!\$)\$(?!\$)(.+?)(?<!\\)\$(?!\$)", _replace_math, text, flags=re.DOTALL)
    return text

def restore_math_as_asciimath(text, math_blocks):
    # For each placeholder, convert the corresponding LaTeX math to AsciiMath and replace
    for i, latex in enumerate(math_blocks):
        try:
            # Strip surrounding $ or \[ \]
            expr = latex.strip()
            if expr.startswith("$$") and expr.endswith("$$"):
                expr = expr[2:-2].strip()
            elif expr.startswith(r"\[") and expr.endswith(r"\]"):
                expr = expr[2:-2].strip()
            elif expr.startswith("$") and expr.endswith("$"):
                expr = expr[1:-1].strip()
            # Convert to AsciiMath
            asciimath = Tex2ASCIIMath(expr)
            # Place as single-line asciimath with delimiters
            text = text.replace(MATH_PLACEHOLDER.format(i), f"`{asciimath}`")
        except Exception as e:
            # Fallback: put original latex back if conversion failed
            text = text.replace(MATH_PLACEHOLDER.format(i), latex)
    return text

def clean_text(text):
    text = html.unescape(text)
    math_blocks = []
    text = extract_and_replace_math(text, math_blocks)
    text = remove_latex_commands(text)
    text = remove_html_and_markdown(text)
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text)
    text = text.strip()
    text = restore_math_as_asciimath(text, math_blocks)
    return text

def stream_json_objects(path):
    with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
        text = f.read()
    match = re.search(r'var\s+jsonData\s*=\s*\[(.*)\];', text, re.DOTALL)
    if not match:
        raise ValueError("Couldn't find jsonData array.")
    array_str = match.group(1).strip()
    objects = []
    parts = re.split(r'\},\s*\{', array_str)
    for i, part in enumerate(parts):
        obj_str = part
        if i == 0:
            obj_str = obj_str + "}"
        elif i == len(parts) - 1:
            obj_str = "{" + obj_str
        else:
            obj_str = "{" + obj_str + "}"
        try:
            obj = json.loads(obj_str)
            objects.append(obj)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Skipped conversation {i} due to error: {e}")
    print(f"Extracted {len(objects)} conversations.")
    return objects

def get_time(ts):
    if not ts:
        return ""
    try:
        return datetime.fromtimestamp(float(ts)).isoformat(timespec='seconds')
    except:
        if isinstance(ts, str) and ts.startswith("20"):
            return ts
        return ""

def format_conversation(conv):
    mapping = conv.get("mapping", {})
    messages = []
    root_id = next((k for k, v in mapping.items() if v.get("parent") is None), None)
    if not root_id:
        return ""
    queue = list(mapping[root_id].get("children", []))
    while queue:
        mid = queue.pop(0)
        msg_obj = mapping.get(mid, {}).get('message')
        if not msg_obj:
            continue
        author = msg_obj.get("author", {})
        role = author.get("role", "")
        if role == "user":
            author_name = "Micah Blumberg"
        elif role in ("assistant", "system") or (author.get("name") and "GPT" in author.get("name")):
            author_name = "Self Aware Networks GPT"
        else:
            author_name = author.get("name", role or "Unknown")
            if author_name:
                lname = author_name.lower()
                if lname.startswith("chatgpt") or lname in {"o3", "gpt-4o", "gpt-4-1", "gpt-4-5"}:
                    author_name = "Self Aware Networks GPT"
        ts = msg_obj.get("create_time", "")
        msg_time = get_time(ts)
        content = msg_obj.get("content", {})
        if content.get("content_type") == "text":
            text = " ".join(content.get("parts", []))
        elif content.get("content_type") == "code":
            text = content.get("text", "")
        elif content.get("content_type") == "thoughts":
            text = " ".join([t.get("summary", "") + " " + t.get("content", "") for t in content.get("thoughts", [])])
        else:
            text = str(content)
        clean = clean_text(text)
        if clean:
            tag = f"{msg_time or 'UNKNOWN_TIME'} {author_name}: {clean}"
            messages.append(tag)
        queue.extend(mapping[mid].get("children", []))
    return "\n\n".join(messages) + "\n\n"

def main():
    input_file = "chat.html"
    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    conversations = stream_json_objects(input_file)
    file_index = 1
    cur_words = 0
    cur_bytes = 0
    output = []
    def save():
        nonlocal output, file_index, cur_words, cur_bytes
        if output:
            fname = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, f"mgpt{file_index:02d}.txt")
            with open(fname, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                text = "\n\n".join(output)
                f.write(text)
            print(f"Wrote {fname} ({cur_words} words, {cur_bytes//1024//1024} MB)")
            output = []
            file_index += 1
            cur_words = 0
            cur_bytes = 0
    for conv in conversations:
        text = format_conversation(conv)
        words = len(text.split())
        bytes_len = len(text.encode("utf-8"))
        if cur_words + words > MAX_WORDS or cur_bytes + bytes_len > MAX_BYTES:
            save()
        output.append(text)
        cur_words += words
        cur_bytes += bytes_len
    save()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
